# C2a safe read commands — 2026-10-10

**C2a accepted after Ibrahim Q8 and independent final review; committed914d9d3.** B2 fixture safety committed `cbb3e2b`. C2a implements recovery-free read projections and commands with private name/settings metadata; C2b deterministic generation of277 screen fixtures remains a separate reviewed boundary. Whole C2 and CP2 are incomplete until fixture generation and native live evidence pass. No live apps/chats, installation, permission changes or push.

## Scope and decisions

Root owns additive ledger read metadata/settings, catalogue and plan records. Builder owns read services/commands/shared serializers and narrow extraction from Linker/Merger/ChatRefresh/UndoPreview; independent agent reviews. No new third-party dependency. Existing historical untracked E9-E10.md stays preserved.

Ibrahim Q6 approved completed-history and merge-preset labels; Q7 approved “Baton cannot use {tool} yet.”. All copy is in the catalogue. Orchestrator split C2 execution into independently green C2a/C2b boundaries to avoid interruption during277-fixture replacement; acceptance scope is unchanged and the execution choice awaits review.

## Root challenge so far

- Read commands must not finalize or recover begun writes, or misimport an unfinished native append as a genuine origin. Scoped pending writes cause a safe read refusal; history projects only committed events.
- Independent/root reviews found all-False history eligibility, PATH-only installation detection, private/unknown messages leaking into status, and a Note object replacing the frozen merge preset label string. Builder is fixing these before acceptance.
- Root found saved setting overrides falsely presented as defaults; actual wiring defaults must be shown.
- Root in-memory mutations bypassed settings validation and tool identity in the name cache. Both meaningful metadata regressions fail; no source mutation was left behind.

### Separate reviewer findings, conditional verdict before final delta

```text
# Independent C2a review — 2026-10-10

Reviewer: separate agent from root/shared-store builder and read-command builder. Read-only repository review, tests on temporary fixtures only; no code edits, commits, live application/chat access or extra agents.

Scope: approved CP2 repair plan section 6, C2 read-command slice, frozen CONTRACT and architecture. C2a is an internal green-boundary decomposition. It does not complete C2: deterministic full fixture generation, manifest/catalogue audits and generated Swift fixture acceptance remain C2b. Native live acceptance and CP2 remain incomplete.

Reviewed services ReadViews/SetupRead/SettingsRead; thin setup/chats/suggestions/links/status/history/merge --show/settings get commands; wiring and serializers; recovery-free UndoPreview extraction; shared merge/suggestion projections; ChatRefresh metadata/automatic-side additions; additive ledger metadata/settings schema and ports; approved history/preset/setup labels; contract/unit tests.

Findings closed in frozen source:
1. Raw status --messages could leak reasoning/unknown kinds. Public for_target mapping now strips them; sentinel regression checks status and merge output.
2. History originally disabled all undo/restore. Separate UndoPreview reuses existing read-only E8 preflight, no recovering constructor. Native boundary/outside-write/expiry tests cover eligibility; an idle release-capable cut uses read-only check_release while a replying chat refuses.
3. Menu/list summary falsely said in sync with no attention while added turns require visibility action. Headline and attention now use explicit needs; relaunch and reopen/mark-seen tests preserve in_sync agent truth without inferred visibility.
4. Merge preset label changed frozen string to Note object. String restored; label_note is additive catalogue evidence. No-conflict merge notes no longer claim a decision is needed.
5. Absent adapter setup had incomplete tool.cannot fields. Approved setup.adapter_unavailable is bound to tool. Unavailable link adapters and stale selections return expected typed refusals instead of internal crashes. Settings default notes no longer describe saved overrides as defaults.
6. Installation previously used PATH alone. Bundled Codex CLI and configured application probes remain distinct from authentication/readiness. Probes are injected in temporary tests; no process is launched by reviewer.
7. All read commands now have meaningful pending-write evidence: all mutating writer methods, runner.ask and application mutations fail on call; actual temporary Claude native bytes/intent and journal remain unchanged. Pending writes are not reimported as origins; relevant refresh projections refuse rather than recover.
8. Public API docstrings and obsolete extraction imports corrected. Initial full check caught a transient missing SequenceMatcher import during builder cleanup; frozen source restored it and passes.

Outstanding at first final-review write:
- F2 historical missing-name coverage: ordinary new Linker create caches refs, but link_summary currently falls back cache -> blank. A pre-C2/brief link with retained ref evidence, disappearing before any C2 read, still needs exact tool/chat historical-ref fallback. The current created-twin test reads links before disappearing and does not prove that case. Root/builder notified; acceptance pending closure.
- Root Q8 approval for completed-only public history versus internal bookkeeping rows was pending. Do not silently accept blank technical rows or false completed delivery labels. Root records the decision/boundary.

Evidence:

Do not release/claim this as finished. Pending decision: show known completed actions only while keeping every underlying record, or obtain accurate labels for internal bookkeeping. Independent final historical-ref delta review is closed; code approval is conditional only on Q8 and its final display regression. Native live adapter gates remain fail/not run, default Codex mutation/automatic closure safely unavailable. No push or real app/chat access.

## Separate final code review and actual challenge evidence

```text
# Independent C2a review — 2026-10-10

Reviewer: separate agent from root/shared-store builder and read-command builder. Read-only repository review, tests on temporary fixtures only; no code edits, commits, live application/chat access or extra agents.

Scope: approved CP2 repair plan section 6, C2 read-command slice, frozen CONTRACT and architecture. C2a is an internal green-boundary decomposition. It does not complete C2: deterministic full fixture generation, manifest/catalogue audits and generated Swift fixture acceptance remain C2b. Native live acceptance and CP2 remain incomplete.

Reviewed services ReadViews/SetupRead/SettingsRead; thin setup/chats/suggestions/links/status/history/merge --show/settings get commands; wiring and serializers; recovery-free UndoPreview extraction; shared merge/suggestion projections; ChatRefresh metadata/automatic-side additions; additive ledger metadata/settings schema and ports; approved history/preset/setup labels; contract/unit tests.

Findings closed in frozen source:
1. Raw status --messages could leak reasoning/unknown kinds. Public for_target mapping now strips them; sentinel regression checks status and merge output.
2. History originally disabled all undo/restore. Separate UndoPreview reuses existing read-only E8 preflight, no recovering constructor. Native boundary/outside-write/expiry tests cover eligibility; an idle release-capable cut uses read-only check_release while a replying chat refuses.
3. Menu/list summary falsely said in sync with no attention while added turns require visibility action. Headline and attention now use explicit needs; relaunch and reopen/mark-seen tests preserve in_sync agent truth without inferred visibility.
4. Merge preset label changed frozen string to Note object. String restored; label_note is additive catalogue evidence. No-conflict merge notes no longer claim a decision is needed.
5. Absent adapter setup had incomplete tool.cannot fields. Approved setup.adapter_unavailable is bound to tool. Unavailable link adapters and stale selections return expected typed refusals instead of internal crashes. Settings default notes no longer describe saved overrides as defaults.
6. Installation previously used PATH alone. Bundled Codex CLI and configured application probes remain distinct from authentication/readiness. Probes are injected in temporary tests; no process is launched by reviewer.
7. All read commands now have meaningful pending-write evidence: all mutating writer methods, runner.ask and application mutations fail on call; actual temporary Claude native bytes/intent and journal remain unchanged. Pending writes are not reimported as origins; relevant refresh projections refuse rather than recover.
8. Public API docstrings and obsolete extraction imports corrected. Initial full check caught a transient missing SequenceMatcher import during builder cleanup; frozen source restored it and passes.

Outstanding at first final-review write:
- F2 historical missing-name coverage: ordinary new Linker create caches refs, but link_summary currently falls back cache -> blank. A pre-C2/brief link with retained ref evidence, disappearing before any C2 read, still needs exact tool/chat historical-ref fallback. The current created-twin test reads links before disappearing and does not prove that case. Root/builder notified; acceptance pending closure.
- Root Q8 approval for completed-only public history versus internal bookkeeping rows was pending. Do not silently accept blank technical rows or false completed delivery labels. Root records the decision/boundary.

## Final narrow F2/refresh verdict

F2 closed: initial brief source metadata now loads only a committed brief event's exact artifact request, keyed by exact source side and current chat identity; matching ref fields validated before private cache. Actual initial brief creation followed by source removal before any C2 read and fresh-engine read preserves the retained source name/folder and committed record/history/native content. No arbitrary cross-tool or cross-chat lookup.

The narrow missing-origin refresh fix is approved: a retained verified complete baseline can compare an already-delivered destination's exact native local ID and public content when the originating chat is unavailable. This is solely confirmation of that existing native copy. It does not populate canonical_sources or authorize a missing-origin delivery payload. Independently challenged matching/tampered/deleted destination cases: matching copy retains shown count; tampered/deleted copy becomes waiting and shown count drops; missing side keeps in_sync=false; all native bytes remain unchanged; a future write preview refuses missing source. An initial challenge mistakenly accessed the recovering Engine.applier under no-effects guard; the guard correctly rejected Journal.recover. Corrected final challenge uses recovery-free PreviewBuilder directly.

Final independent frozen suite:147focused (25 C2 read tests +119 C1/contract tests +3 metadata tests),758 full engine pass. The earlier arithmetic wording is superseded by actual pasted logs. C2a code has no remaining review blocker; final acceptance is CONDITIONAL ONLY on Ibrahim Q8's public history display decision and the exact approved filtering delta's test. Until approval/filtering, do not accept/commit C2a with blank unknown history rows. C2b fixture generation and native/live CP2 evidence remain outstanding regardless of C2a approval.

matching chat_shows 1 waiting 0 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
tampered chat_shows 0 waiting 1 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
deleted chat_shows 0 waiting 1 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
PASS: verified matching native local identity preserves prior delivery; changed/deleted native copy resets delivery; retained baseline does not authorize a missing-origin write.

## Q8 closure and final acceptance

Ibrahim chose to show bookkeeping with new labels. Root implemented distinct catalogue labels for recognizing existing turns, queuing attachment, loss of confirmation, and explicit user confirmation of removal visibility; singular/plural counts read naturally. Unknown future kinds keep their identity and get a neutral label. No event is hidden/deleted, no private detail is emitted, and no bookkeeping entry gains Undo/Restore eligibility. The three new regression tests drive actual alignment/attachment/bypass and undo/mark-seen services, preserve native and ledger records across history reads, and keep unknown future data private.

A separate reviewer approved the final delta and full C2a with no remaining findings. The internal C2a/C2b scheduling split was also checked; whole C2/generated-fixture/live acceptance is unchanged. ### Final independent review (prior conditional verdicts are historical)

```text
# Independent C2a review — 2026-10-10

Reviewer: separate agent from root/shared-store builder and read-command builder. Read-only repository review, tests on temporary fixtures only; no code edits, commits, live application/chat access or extra agents.

Scope: approved CP2 repair plan section 6, C2 read-command slice, frozen CONTRACT and architecture. C2a is an internal green-boundary decomposition. It does not complete C2: deterministic full fixture generation, manifest/catalogue audits and generated Swift fixture acceptance remain C2b. Native live acceptance and CP2 remain incomplete.

Reviewed services ReadViews/SetupRead/SettingsRead; thin setup/chats/suggestions/links/status/history/merge --show/settings get commands; wiring and serializers; recovery-free UndoPreview extraction; shared merge/suggestion projections; ChatRefresh metadata/automatic-side additions; additive ledger metadata/settings schema and ports; approved history/preset/setup labels; contract/unit tests.

Findings closed in frozen source:
1. Raw status --messages could leak reasoning/unknown kinds. Public for_target mapping now strips them; sentinel regression checks status and merge output.
2. History originally disabled all undo/restore. Separate UndoPreview reuses existing read-only E8 preflight, no recovering constructor. Native boundary/outside-write/expiry tests cover eligibility; an idle release-capable cut uses read-only check_release while a replying chat refuses.
3. Menu/list summary falsely said in sync with no attention while added turns require visibility action. Headline and attention now use explicit needs; relaunch and reopen/mark-seen tests preserve in_sync agent truth without inferred visibility.
4. Merge preset label changed frozen string to Note object. String restored; label_note is additive catalogue evidence. No-conflict merge notes no longer claim a decision is needed.
5. Absent adapter setup had incomplete tool.cannot fields. Approved setup.adapter_unavailable is bound to tool. Unavailable link adapters and stale selections return expected typed refusals instead of internal crashes. Settings default notes no longer describe saved overrides as defaults.
6. Installation previously used PATH alone. Bundled Codex CLI and configured application probes remain distinct from authentication/readiness. Probes are injected in temporary tests; no process is launched by reviewer.
7. All read commands now have meaningful pending-write evidence: all mutating writer methods, runner.ask and application mutations fail on call; actual temporary Claude native bytes/intent and journal remain unchanged. Pending writes are not reimported as origins; relevant refresh projections refuse rather than recover.
8. Public API docstrings and obsolete extraction imports corrected. Initial full check caught a transient missing SequenceMatcher import during builder cleanup; frozen source restored it and passes.

Outstanding at first final-review write:
- F2 historical missing-name coverage: ordinary new Linker create caches refs, but link_summary currently falls back cache -> blank. A pre-C2/brief link with retained ref evidence, disappearing before any C2 read, still needs exact tool/chat historical-ref fallback. The current created-twin test reads links before disappearing and does not prove that case. Root/builder notified; acceptance pending closure.
- Root Q8 approval for completed-only public history versus internal bookkeeping rows was pending. Do not silently accept blank technical rows or false completed delivery labels. Root records the decision/boundary.

## Final narrow F2/refresh verdict

F2 closed: initial brief source metadata now loads only a committed brief event's exact artifact request, keyed by exact source side and current chat identity; matching ref fields validated before private cache. Actual initial brief creation followed by source removal before any C2 read and fresh-engine read preserves the retained source name/folder and committed record/history/native content. No arbitrary cross-tool or cross-chat lookup.

The narrow missing-origin refresh fix is approved: a retained verified complete baseline can compare an already-delivered destination's exact native local ID and public content when the originating chat is unavailable. This is solely confirmation of that existing native copy. It does not populate canonical_sources or authorize a missing-origin delivery payload. Independently challenged matching/tampered/deleted destination cases: matching copy retains shown count; tampered/deleted copy becomes waiting and shown count drops; missing side keeps in_sync=false; all native bytes remain unchanged; a future write preview refuses missing source. An initial challenge mistakenly accessed the recovering Engine.applier under no-effects guard; the guard correctly rejected Journal.recover. Corrected final challenge uses recovery-free PreviewBuilder directly.

Final independent frozen suite:147focused (25 C2 read tests +119 C1/contract tests +3 metadata tests),758 full engine pass. The earlier arithmetic wording is superseded by actual pasted logs. C2a code has no remaining review blocker; final acceptance is CONDITIONAL ONLY on Ibrahim Q8's public history display decision and the exact approved filtering delta's test. Until approval/filtering, do not accept/commit C2a with blank unknown history rows. C2b fixture generation and native/live CP2 evidence remain outstanding regardless of C2a approval.

matching chat_shows 1 waiting 0 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
tampered chat_shows 0 waiting 1 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
deleted chat_shows 0 waiting 1 in_sync False future_write_preview_refused KeyError native_unchanged True
PASS: verified matching native local identity preserves prior delivery; changed/deleted native copy resets delivery; retained baseline does not authorize a missing-origin write.

## FINAL ACCEPTANCE — Ibrahim Q8 delta, 2026-10-10

This verdict supersedes every earlier conditional/pending verdict above: **C2a APPROVED. No outstanding C2a review findings.**

Ibrahim explicitly chose to show bookkeeping with new labels (Q8). Root's final catalogue/output delta implements that choice while retaining all raw history records and original event identities. Aligned records describe recognized existing turns, not a new append. Queued attachments remain queued, not delivered. Lost confirmations make no successful-sync claim. Undo display confirmation describes explicit user evidence, not another undo. Unknown future kinds receive the neutral catalogue label “Recorded a history entry”, never blank text or private detail contents. No read-side action eligibility is invented or altered.

Reviewed three actual-service regressions: initial matching-chat alignment then queued attachments and removed-native-content reset; real undo then mark_seen visibility evidence; stored future-kind private detail with neutral public label. Assertions preserve raw history IDs/order/records and native chats after reading, check disabled bookkeeping controls, distinguish all approved semantic labels, and prohibit secret detail disclosure. Independent singular/plural serializer challenge verifies count labels, raw Event immutability, unchanged flags and absent private details. Singular variants preserve the approved meaning.

**Execution split reviewed and approved as an internal Orchestrator decision**, not attributed to Ibrahim/reviewer as a product-spec choice: C2a read-command/projection boundary can be committed green now; C2b separately completes deterministic manifest/generation of the existing277 fixture files and generated Swift acceptance. C2 overall remains incomplete until both pass. This decomposition changes scheduling/checkpoint reporting only, does not waive any original C2 or CP2 gate and does not promote deferred action/hook/relaunch packages. Root should record “Orchestrator, independently reviewed by separate reviewer agent” for this execution decision.

Final independent verification: **150 focused tests pass (28 C2 read +119 C1/contract +3 metadata),761 engine tests pass**. App build/143 app tests supplied by root remains green; no app code changed in final delta. No code edits/Git writes/live apps/chats/extra agents by reviewer. All dirty C2a work and the historical untracked E9-E10.md draft preserved.
